Add-ons
Add-ons add commands to Tools › Add-ons and the command box, and can run when a file is opened or saved. They can do what a script can, and no more: nothing outside the open files, and no network. A new add-on is off until you turn it on here, and one whose file changes is turned off until you look at it again.
Add-ons that are on list their commands here. Manage add-ons turns them on.

TPC
TR-DOS archive (SCL)
TR-DOS disk (TRD) · {}
TRAP #1: QDOS's manager traps (jobs, memory, the clock), the key in d0.
TRAP #2: QDOS's traps that open and close channels, the key in d0.
TRAP #3: QDOS's input and output on the channel in a0, the key in d0 and the timeout in d3.
TRAP #4: makes the next TRAP #2 or #3's addresses relative to a6, as SuperBASIC's procedures need.
TRAP #{trap}: not one QDOS uses for its calls (1 the manager's, 2 opening channels, 3 input and output, 4 a6-relative).
TRAP takes 0 to 15, and {n} isn't
